# STD Stage 0 inheritance benchmark

Date 2026-10-20T16:45:58.073Z, commit `2c04d17`, command `pnpm bench`, seed 20261020, total time 70 min 12 s.

Method (Contracts 2.6): 10 warm-up and 200 measured runs per decisive read (5 and 30 for the supplementary ones) with seeded random parameters, run one at a time on one connection; p50 and p95 in milliseconds measured at the client; `EXPLAIN (ANALYZE, BUFFERS)` of the median run; writes with 16 clients for 60 s each. Stage 0 queries are hand-written SQL mirroring what STD's compiler will generate (`bench/src/compile.ts`). Reproduce with `pnpm bench`.

## Decision

**Outcome: reopen.** Class-table storage does not satisfy the decision rule, with or without the class_id index mitigations. The other two models were benchmarked and the storage decision is reopened: Stage 0 stops here for Martin's decision. class-table @ 10m misses Q6 (265.527 ms > 50 ms), Q7 (158.242 ms > 50 ms); the baseline is not more than 2x faster on any read query (vs single-table @ 10m). Rule: not met. class-table + class_id indexes @ 10m misses Q3 (78.509 ms > 50 ms), Q4 (50.756 ms > 50 ms), Q6 (267.428 ms > 50 ms), Q7 (166.25 ms > 50 ms); the baseline is more than 2x faster on 1 read query: Q3 (2.534x) (vs single-table @ 10m). Rule: not met.

### Concurrent load

## Definitions

### Hierarchy (Contracts 2.6)

| Class | Level | Parent | Own fields | Records | Fields |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| bench.item | L1 |  | 12 | 10% | name text, number text, status text, owner uuid (1% null), location text, priority integer, cost numeric(12,2) (5% null), purchased_on date (10% null), active boolean, description text (30% null), last_seen_at timestamptz(3) (20% null), external_ref uuid (50% null) |
| bench.device | L2 | bench.item | 8 | 5% | serial_number text, manufacturer text, model text, acquired_on date, warranty_until date (20% null), rated_watts integer, managed boolean, asset_tag bigint |
| bench.furniture | L2 | bench.item | 8 | 10% | material text, color text, width_cm integer, height_cm integer, depth_cm integer, weight_kg numeric(8,2), assembled boolean, room text (10% null) |
| bench.license | L2 | bench.item | 8 | 10% | product text, vendor text, seats integer, license_key text, valid_from date, valid_until date (30% null), cost_per_seat numeric(10,2), perpetual boolean |
| bench.computer | L3 | bench.device | 8 | 5% | hostname text, os text, cpu_cores integer, ram_gb integer, disk_gb integer, bios_version text, domain_joined boolean, last_patched_at timestamptz(3) (10% null) |
| bench.network | L3 | bench.device | 8 | 10% | mac_address text, ip_address text, port_count integer, vlan integer, firmware text, throughput_bps bigint, poe boolean, rack text (15% null) |
| bench.peripheral | L3 | bench.device | 8 | 15% | peripheral_type text, interface text, color text, dpi integer (50% null), wireless boolean, battery_pct integer (40% null), paired_at timestamptz(3) (30% null), vendor_sku text |
| bench.laptop | L4 | bench.computer | 6 | 25% | screen_inches numeric(4,1), battery_cycles integer, cpu_model text, classification text, encrypted boolean, last_docked_at timestamptz(3) (30% null) |
| bench.server | L4 | bench.computer | 6 | 10% | rack_unit integer, cluster text, virtualized boolean, power_supplies integer, uptime_days integer, commissioned_on date |

`bench.ticket` has 20% as many rows as the hierarchy; fields: number, short_description, state, priority, item, opened_by, confidential, opened_at; `item` references a random record. 50,000 users (2,500 in smoke mode) in `core.user`, referenced from `item.owner` (1% null) and the audit fields. Every record ID comes from `uuidv7()`.

### Callers

- **admin**: the built-in allow-all rule.
- **restricted**: rows where `owner = me OR status = "shared"`; `item.location` denied when `priority > 8` (compiled as `CASE WHEN <readable> THEN location END` in selection, filters, sorts and cursors); `bench.laptop` records denied when `classification = "restricted"` (applies per class through `class_id`; class-table joins `bench.laptop` for it); tickets where `confidential = false OR opened_by = me`; the item reference in Q7 joins with the item's visibility as a guard. `me` is a random user (the record's owner for the restricted Q5 and W2).

### Requests

| Read | Caller | Endpoint | Decisive | Definition |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Q1 | admin | item | yes | L1: status = s AND location = l (0.5%), sort updated_at desc, first 50 |
| Q2 | admin | item | yes | L1: status in 3 values (30%), sort updated_at desc, page of 50 at keyset depth 10,000, with count |
| Q3 | admin | laptop | yes | L4: laptop.cpu_model = c (1%) AND item.active = b (50%), sort device.warranty_until asc, 50 |
| Q4 | admin | computer | yes | L3: computer.os = o (0.5%), sort item.cost desc, 50 |
| Q5.L1 | admin | item | yes | L1: get by id (any class), class resolved, owner label |
| Q5.L4 | admin | laptop | yes | L4: get a laptop by id with all four levels, owner label |
| Q6 | restricted | item | yes | Q1 as the restricted caller (row rule, masked location, laptop rule) |
| Q7 | restricted | ticket | yes | ticket: item.location = l through the reference (masked, guarded join), sort updated_at desc, 50 |
| Q2 restricted | restricted | item | no | Q2 as the restricted caller |
| Q3 restricted | restricted | laptop | no | Q3 as the restricted caller |
| Q4 restricted | restricted | computer | no | Q4 as the restricted caller |
| Q5.L1 restricted | restricted | item | no | Q5.L1 as the record's owner |
| Q5.L4 restricted | restricted | laptop | no | Q5.L4 as the laptop's owner |
| Q7 admin | admin | ticket | no | Q7 as the admin |

Every list selects every readable field of the endpoint, `class_id` and the owner label (Q7: the item and opener labels), and breaks ties by `id`. Q2's cursor is the 10,000th row of the same request (found by an unmeasured probe); the cursor predicate is the expanded null-aware form of Contracts 3.5 plus the redundant bound on the first key. Each read runs in its own READ COMMITTED transaction, as STD's request transactions do. Statements are prepared (the driver's default), so Postgres may switch to a generic plan after five executions; the reported plan is an `EXPLAIN ANALYZE` of the median run's parameters.

- **W1**: insert a laptop through `/bench.laptop` (class-table: root row, then device, computer and laptop rows).
- **W2**: update `laptop.battery_cycles`: lock the root row alone `FOR NO KEY UPDATE`, read the existing state (all levels, visibility) in the next statement, update the level row, bump the root version with a version check.
- **W3**: delete a laptop through `/bench.item`: lock the root `FOR UPDATE`, read the existing state of the concrete class, delete the root row with a version check (level rows cascade). Deletes the laptops W1 created.
- The restricted caller's writes add the existing-state and proposed-state checks through the compiled predicates; writes its rules refuse are counted separately and not timed.


```sql
SELECT i.id::text AS id, i.class_id AS class_id, i.version AS version, i.created_at AS created_at, i.created_by AS created_by, i.updated_at AS updated_at, i.updated_by AS updated_by, i."name" AS "name", i."number" AS "number", i."status" AS "status", i."owner" AS "owner", CASE WHEN (i."priority" <= $5::integer OR i."priority" IS NULL) THEN i."location" END AS "location", COALESCE((i."priority" <= $5::integer OR i."priority" IS NULL), false) AS "location$readable", i."priority" AS "priority", i."cost" AS "cost", i."purchased_on" AS "purchased_on", i."active" AS "active", i."description" AS "description", i."last_seen_at" AS "last_seen_at", i."external_ref" AS "external_ref", ou.name AS "owner$label" FROM "bench"."item" i LEFT JOIN "core"."user" ou ON ou.id = i."owner" WHERE ((i.class_id IN ('0e5b0000-0000-7000-8000-000000000001'::uuid, '0e5b0000-0000-7000-8000-000000000002'::uuid, '0e5b0000-0000-7000-8000-000000000003'::uuid, '0e5b0000-0000-7000-8000-000000000004'::uuid, '0e5b0000-0000-7000-8000-000000000005'::uuid, '0e5b0000-0000-7000-8000-000000000006'::uuid, '0e5b0000-0000-7000-8000-000000000007'::uuid, '0e5b0000-0000-7000-8000-000000000009'::uuid) AND (i."owner" = $1::uuid OR i."status" = $2::text)) OR (i.class_id = '0e5b0000-0000-7000-8000-000000000008'::uuid AND (i."owner" = $1::uuid OR i."status" = $2::text) AND (i."classification" <> $3::text OR i."classification" IS NULL))) AND i."status" = ANY($4::text[]) ORDER BY i.updated_at DESC NULLS FIRST, i.id DESC NULLS FIRST LIMIT 50
```

```sql
SELECT i.id::text AS id, i.class_id AS class_id, i.version AS version, i.created_at AS created_at, i.created_by AS created_by, i.updated_at AS updated_at, i.updated_by AS updated_by, i."name" AS "name", i."number" AS "number", i."status" AS "status", i."owner" AS "owner", CASE WHEN (i."priority" <= $6::integer OR i."priority" IS NULL) THEN i."location" END AS "location", COALESCE((i."priority" <= $6::integer OR i."priority" IS NULL), false) AS "location$readable", i."priority" AS "priority", i."cost" AS "cost", i."purchased_on" AS "purchased_on", i."active" AS "active", i."description" AS "description", i."last_seen_at" AS "last_seen_at", i."external_ref" AS "external_ref", i."serial_number" AS "serial_number", i."manufacturer" AS "manufacturer", i."model" AS "model", i."acquired_on" AS "acquired_on", i."warranty_until" AS "warranty_until", i."rated_watts" AS "rated_watts", i."managed" AS "managed", i."asset_tag" AS "asset_tag", i."hostname" AS "hostname", i."os" AS "os", i."cpu_cores" AS "cpu_cores", i."ram_gb" AS "ram_gb", i."disk_gb" AS "disk_gb", i."bios_version" AS "bios_version", i."domain_joined" AS "domain_joined", i."last_patched_at" AS "last_patched_at", i."screen_inches" AS "screen_inches", i."battery_cycles" AS "battery_cycles", i."cpu_model" AS "cpu_model", i."classification" AS "classification", i."encrypted" AS "encrypted", i."last_docked_at" AS "last_docked_at", ou.name AS "owner$label" FROM "bench"."item" i LEFT JOIN "core"."user" ou ON ou.id = i."owner" WHERE i.class_id = '0e5b0000-0000-7000-8000-000000000008'::uuid AND ((i."owner" = $1::uuid OR i."status" = $2::text) AND (i."classification" <> $3::text OR i."classification" IS NULL)) AND i."cpu_model" = $4::text AND i."active" = $5::boolean ORDER BY i."warranty_until" ASC NULLS LAST, i.id ASC NULLS LAST LIMIT 50
```
